## 🤔 Seedance 2.0 क्या है?

**Seedance 2.0** **ByteDance** द्वारा विकसित एक वीडियो जनरेशन मॉडल है। यह उद्योग का पहला मॉडल है जो **चार-मोडल एक साथ इनपुट** का समर्थन करता है — छवि, वीडियो, ऑडियो और टेक्स्ट।

**Key Features:**
- 🎥 **टेक्स्ट से वीडियो** — टेक्स्ट विवरण से वीडियो जनरेट करें
- 🖼️ **छवि से वीडियो** — स्थिर छवियों को गतिशील वीडियो में एनिमेट करें
- 📹 **वीडियो से वीडियो** — मौजूदा वीडियो को रूपांतरित और विस्तारित करें
- 🎵 **ऑडियो-चालित** — ऑडियो इनपुट द्वारा संचालित वीडियो जनरेट करें
- 📐 **1080p रिज़ॉल्यूशन तक**, 4-15 सेकंड अवधि
- 🔊 **ऑटो डबिंग और स्कोरिंग** — स्वचालित वॉयसओवर और बैकग्राउंड म्यूजिक

## 🔥 विशेष प्रॉम्पट्स

> ⭐ हाथ से चुने गए उच्च गुणवत्ता वाले प्रॉम्प्ट

### No. 1: सीडान्स 2.0: 15-सेकंड की सिनेमैटिक जापानी रोमांस शॉर्ट फिल्म

![中文](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-中文-red)
![Featured](https://img.shields.io/badge/⭐-Featured-gold)

#### 📖 विवरण

सीडेंस 2.0 (Seedance 2.0) के लिए एक अत्यधिक विस्तृत, 15-सेकंड का मल्टी-सीन प्रॉम्प्ट, जिसे एक सिनेमैटिक, अल्ट्रा-रियलिस्टिक जापानी हाई स्कूल की शुद्ध प्रेम लघु फिल्म बनाने के लिए डिज़ाइन किया गया है। प्रॉम्प्ट में दृश्य सेटिंग (खाली क्लासरूम, गर्म सुनहरी धूप, धूल के कण), कैमरा मूवमेंट, चरित्र की निरंतरता (कोई विकृति/बदलाव नहीं), सूक्ष्म माइक्रो-एक्सप्रेशंस, सिंक्रनाइज़्ड सांस लेना/होंठों की हरकत, संवाद और ध्वनि प्रभाव (टिड्डियों की आवाज़, पेन की खरोंच, कम-आवृत्ति वाली दिल की धड़कन, नरम पियानो) निर्दिष्ट हैं। कथा एक लड़की के लिखने और एक लड़के के उसे चुपचाप देखने के बीच के तीव्र, अजीब और अंतरंग भावनात्मक तनाव पर केंद्रित है, जिसका समापन एक शर्मीले टकराव में होता है।

#### 📝 प्रॉम्पट

```
15-सेकंड की सिनेमैटिक जापानी ड्रामा शुद्ध प्रेम अस्पष्ट लघु फिल्म, अति-यथार्थवादी गुणवत्ता, दोपहर में एक खाली कक्षा में गर्म सुनहरी धूप, खिड़कियों से होकर अगल-बगल रखी डेस्क पर फैल रही है, महीन धूल के कण प्रकाश की किरणों में धीरे-धीरे तैर रहे हैं, पुरानी लकड़ी की डेस्क, अत्यंत स्वाभाविक सूक्ष्म हरकतें, साँस लेना और आँखों का तनाव, पात्रों के चेहरे, कपड़े और केशविन्यास बिना किसी विकृति, बहाव या कलाकृतियों के लगातार बने रहते हैं, साँस लेने के साथ छाती का वास्तविक हल्का उठना और गिरना, उथली गहराई, मलाईदार धुंधली पृष्ठभूमि, गर्म फिल्म ग्रेन, 8K शार्प, जापानी युवाओं का संयमित दिल दहला देने वाला दम घोंटने वाला माहौल।
0-4 सेकंड: डेस्कटॉप के मध्यम शॉट से दो लोगों के अगल-बगल बैठे हुए क्लोज-अप तक एक अत्यंत धीमा पुश-इन शॉट। गर्मी की स्कूल यूनिफॉर्म में एक शुद्ध लड़की सिर झुकाकर नोट्स लिखने में तल्लीन है, उसके लंबे काले बाल और कानों के पास के बिखरे बाल हल्की हवा से धीरे से उठ रहे हैं, लंबी पलकें सूक्ष्म छाया डाल रही हैं, त्वचा स्वाभाविक रूप से गुलाबी और कोमल है, एकाग्रता में उसके मुँह के कोने में एक हल्का, अनजाने में ऊपर की ओर मुड़ना, हल्की और समान साँस लेना।
4-9 सेकंड: लड़के के क्लोज-अप पर स्विच करें। उसकी स्कूल यूनिफॉर्म का कॉलर थोड़ा ढीला है, वह अपनी कोहनी डेस्क पर टिकाता है और चुपके से अपना सिर घुमाकर उसे देखता है, उसकी आँखें कोमल, संयमित स्नेह और कोमलता से भरी हैं, पुतलियाँ थोड़ी फैली हुई हैं, उसका एडम का सेब धीरे से घूमता है। अचानक उसकी कलम रुकते हुए देखकर, वह जल्दी और घबराकर अपना सिर घुमाकर अपने नोट्स देखने का नाटक करता है, उसके कान के लोब जल्दी से थोड़े लाल हो जाते हैं, कलम पकड़ते समय उसकी उंगलियाँ थोड़ी काँपती हैं, कभी-कभी अपनी बैंग्स के नीचे से उसे देखता है, उसकी साँस थोड़ी अव्यवस्थित है, और शांत रहने के प्रयास में उसके होंठ कसकर दबे हुए हैं।
9-15 सेकंड: एक ही फ्रेम में दोनों चेहरों का एक्सट्रीम क्लोज-अप, धीमी गति में आँखें अचानक मिलती हैं: लड़की धीरे-धीरे अपना सिर घुमाती है, पहले एक चकित आश्चर्य दिखाती है, फिर जल्दी और शरमाकर 0.3 सेकंड के लिए अपना सिर नीचे करती है, धीरे से अपने निचले होंठ को काटती है, उसके गाल और कान के लोब तुरंत चेरी ब्लॉसम गुलाबी रंग में खिल उठते हैं, उसकी नम पलकें डरपोक होकर ऊपर उठती हैं ताकि उसकी नज़र से फिर से मिल सकें, जबकि धीरे और शरमाकर फुसफुसाती है, "...क्या देख रहे हो?"; लड़का पूरी तरह से जम जाता है, उसकी पुतलियाँ फैल जाती हैं, और वह 0.4 सेकंड के लिए स्तब्ध रह जाता है, फिर घबराकर और चुपचाप हकलाते हुए जवाब देता है, "क-कुछ नहीं..."। लड़की और भी धीमी आवाज़ में फुसफुसाती है, अपने होंठ काटती है और उसे फिर से देखती है, फुसफुसाती रहती है, "...झूठे।" लड़का रुकता है, फिर धीरे से आह भरता है और फुसफुसाता है, "...बस तुम्हें देख रहा हूँ।" उसके मुँह का कोना धीरे-धीरे एक शर्मीली, कोमल, टेढ़ी मुस्कान में ऊपर की ओर मुड़ता है, उसकी आँखों के कोनों पर महीन रेखाएँ दिखाई देती हैं, और उसकी साँस लेने की गति स्पष्ट रूप से गहरी हो जाती है। एक अदृश्य धारा उनके चेहरों के बीच अस्पष्ट तनाव को खींचती हुई प्रतीत होती है, एक-दूसरे के साँस लेने का तापमान साझा करती है, पृष्ठभूमि पूरी तरह से मलाईदार, स्वप्निल प्रकाश धब्बों, गर्म प्रभामंडल और महीन हवा के कणों की परतों में पिघल जाती है।
होंठों का तालमेल स्वाभाविक और सटीक है, भावनात्मक सूक्ष्म-कंपन और साँस लेना सिंक्रनाइज़ हैं, संवाद एक शर्मीली आवाज़ में कम-ऊर्जा वाली फुसफुसाहट है, 200-400 मिलीसेकंड के बीच स्वाभाविक छोटे विराम, बोलते समय मुँह केवल थोड़ा हिलता है, बिना अतिशयोक्ति या रोबोटिक महसूस के, सही प्राकृतिक लिप-सिंक और भावनात्मक प्रामाणिकता।
समग्र ध्वनि प्रभाव: दूर से गर्मियों में झींगुरों की हल्की आवाज़, कागज पर कलम के छूने की नरम खरोंच की आवाज़, उनके दिल की धड़कनों की लगभग अश्रव्य कम-आवृत्ति वाली धड़कन, अंत में एक बहुत ही हल्के, हवादार पियानो में फीकी पड़ जाती है। संवाद पूरी तरह से स्वाभाविक रूप से फुसफुसाहट के रूप में दृश्य में एकीकृत है, लड़की की आवाज़ नरम और शर्मीली है, लड़का घबराकर हकलाने से कोमल में बदल जाता है।
चरित्र की पहचान पूरे समय बनी रहती है, वास्तविक सूक्ष्म सिर झुकाना, आँखों की हरकतें और साँस लेने का तालमेल, कोई टेक्स्ट, वॉटरमार्क या उपशीर्षक नहीं, शुद्ध जापानी शैली का युवा गुप्त क्रश दिल दहला देने वाला सस्पेंस।
```

### No. 2: हॉलीवुड हाउते कॉउचर फ़ैंटेसी वीडियो प्रॉम्प्ट

![中文](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-中文-red)
![Featured](https://img.shields.io/badge/⭐-Featured-gold)

#### 📖 विवरण

सीडेंस 2.0 के लिए एक विस्तृत, मल्टी-सीन वीडियो जनरेशन प्रॉम्प्ट, जिसे हॉलीवुड हाउते कॉउचर फ़ैंटेसी फ़िल्म बनाने के लिए डिज़ाइन किया गया है। प्रॉम्प्ट में स्टाइल, रिज़ॉल्यूशन (8K), रेंडरिंग इंजन (अनरियल इंजन 5), अवधि (15 सेकंड) और तरल नीले-सफेद पोर्सिलेन में एक मॉडल से जुड़े तीन अलग-अलग कैमरा/एक्शन सीक्वेंस निर्दिष्ट किए गए हैं, जो स्याही-धुले निगल में टूट जाते हैं, और 3D तरल स्याही के भंवर में समाप्त होते हैं।

#### 📝 प्रॉम्पट

```
[शैली] हॉलीवुड हाउते कॉउचर फ़ैंटेसी ब्लॉकबस्टर, 8K अल्ट्रा-क्लियर, फ़ोटोरियलिस्टिक, हाई-फ़ैशन एडिटोरियल स्टाइल, अनरियल इंजन 5 फ़्लूइड रेंडरिंग, विज़ुअल इल्यूज़न। [अवधि] 15 सेकंड। [दृश्य] एक अंतहीन, वास्तविक सालार दे उयूनी (स्काई मिरर) नमक का मैदान। आसमान दमनकारी काले बादलों से भरा है, और ज़मीन हर चीज़ को एक दर्पण की तरह पूरी तरह से दर्शाती है, जिसमें समग्र चित्र एक न्यूनतम, कूल टोन प्रस्तुत करता है। [00:00-00:05] शॉट 1: हाउते कॉउचर प्रवेश और चीनी मिट्टी जैसी त्वचा। कैमरा स्थिति: अत्यधिक निम्न-कोण ऊपर की ओर शॉट, अल्ट्रा-टेलीफोटो लेंस ज़ूम-इन। क्रिया: एक एशियाई महिला मॉडल, जिसकी पहचान योग्य, हाई-फ़ैशन चेहरा है, पानी की सतह पर ठंडे अंदाज़ में चलती है। प्रभाव: उसने कपड़े नहीं, बल्कि बहते हुए, असली लिक्विड ब्लू-एंड-व्हाइट पोर्सिलेन से बनी एक लंबी पोशाक पहनी हुई है। जैसे ही वह चलती है, स्कर्ट असली सिरेमिक की तरह एक कुरकुरी टक्कर की आवाज़ करती है, जिसकी सतह पर एक बहती हुई चमक होती है। पारंपरिक नीले और सफेद पैटर्न सफेद चीनी मिट्टी जैसी बनावट वाली स्कर्ट पर ऐसे चलते हैं जैसे वे जीवित हों। [00:05-00:10] शॉट 2: भौतिक विखंडन और इंक-वॉश अवतरण। कैमरा स्थिति: चेहरे का अत्यधिक क्लोज-अप, फ़ोकस तेज़ी से पीछे हटता है। क्रिया: मॉडल अचानक रुक जाती है, कैमरे को ठंडे अंदाज़ में घूरती है, और अपनी उंगलियों को कुरकुरी आवाज़ के साथ चटकाती है। प्रभाव: जैसे ही उंगलियाँ चटकती हैं, उसकी नीली और सफेद पोर्सिलेन पोशाक गिरती नहीं है, बल्कि तुरंत हज़ारों अत्यधिक फ़ोटोरियलिस्टिक इंक-वॉश स्वैलोज़ में फट जाती है। ये स्वैलोज़ असली पानी की बूंदें और स्याही के निशान ले जाते हैं, हवा में काले तरल पदार्थ के बादलों को खींचते हुए, उसके चारों ओर तेज़ी से घूमते हैं। [00:10-00:15] शॉट 3: आयामी विघटन और अगाध प्रतिबिंब। कैमरा स्थिति: उच्च-ऊंचाई वाला ओवरहेड शॉट, कैमरा तेज़ी से घूमता और नीचे उतरता है। क्रिया: इंक-वॉश स्वैलोज़ का झुंड मॉडल के पैरों के नीचे दर्पण जैसे झील के पानी में गोता लगाता है। प्रभाव: मूल रूप से ठोस नमक झील का सतही तनाव तुरंत गायब हो जाता है। पूरा अत्यधिक यथार्थवादी संसार केंद्रित स्याही को साफ पानी में गिराने की तरह हिंसक रूप से रक्तस्राव और घुलना शुरू कर देता है। असली काले बादल और मॉडल की आकृति पूरी तरह से एक अत्यंत भव्य 3D फ़्लूइड इंक वर्टेक्स में बदल जाती है, जो कैमरे को एक काले और सफेद आपस में गुंथे हुए अगाध में पूरी तरह से निगल जाती है।
```

### No. 3: आधुनिक ग्रामीण सौंदर्य चिकित्सा लघु फिल्म वीडियो प्रॉम्प्ट

![中文](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-中文-red)
![Featured](https://img.shields.io/badge/⭐-Featured-gold)

#### 📖 विवरण

सीडांस 2.0 (Seedance 2.0) के लिए एक विस्तृत, तीन-शॉट प्रॉम्प्ट, जो मॉडर्न रूरल एस्थेटिक्स (Modern Rural Aesthetics) शैली में एक सुकून देने वाली, सिनेमैटिक लघु फिल्म बनाने के लिए है। यह शैली (सिनेमैटिक कमर्शियल, 4K/8K, एक्सट्रीम मैक्रो, प्राकृतिक प्रकाश, ASMR), सेटिंग (बगीचे के दृश्य वाली एक आधुनिक खुली रसोई), चरित्र (लिनन में एक केंद्रित निर्माता), और तीन दृश्यों के लिए विशिष्ट क्रियाएँ निर्दिष्ट करता है: एक टमाटर चुनना, सटीक कटिंग, और खाने का एक शांत क्षण।

#### 📝 प्रॉम्पट

```
[शैली]
आधुनिक ग्रामीण सौंदर्यशास्त्र, सिनेमैटिक कमर्शियल गुणवत्ता, Sony A7S3/सिनेमा कैमरे से शूट किया गया, 4K/8K अल्ट्रा-क्लियर, एक्सट्रीम मैक्रो, प्राकृतिक पारदर्शी प्रकाश व्यवस्था, हीलिंग ASMR, कोई ऐतिहासिक कॉस्ट्यूम ड्रामा जैसा अनुभव नहीं।

[दृश्य]
एक अच्छी तरह से बनाए रखा गया आधुनिक फार्महाउस की खुली रसोई, पृष्ठभूमि में एक हरा-भरा सब्जी का बगीचा, तेज धूप।

[चरित्र]
आधुनिक ग्रामीण क्रिएटर, काले लंबे बाल लकड़ी की हेयरपिन से लापरवाही से बंधे हुए, गहरे नीले रंग के आरामदायक लिनन के कपड़े पहने हुए, हल्का मेकअप, केंद्रित और शांत आँखें।

[शॉट विवरण]
[00:00-00:05] शॉट 1: सुबह की कटाई (ताजगी)
दृश्य: हाई-डेफिनिशन क्लोज-अप। सुबह की धूप पौधों पर साइड बैकलाइटिंग के साथ पड़ती है।
एक्शन: क्रिएटर के नंगे हाथ (लंबी, साफ उंगलियां) बेल से ओस की बूंदों से चमकता हुआ एक चमकीला लाल टमाटर तोड़ते हैं।
विवरण: अत्यधिक तीक्ष्ण फोकस, टमाटर की सतह पर रोमिलता और फिसलती हुई पानी की बूंदों का प्रक्षेपवक्र स्पष्ट रूप से दिखाई दे रहा है। पृष्ठभूमि धुंधली उच्च-गुणवत्ता वाली हरी है।

[00:05-00:10] शॉट 2: अत्यधिक कारीगरी (शिल्प)
दृश्य: इनडोर स्टोव क्षेत्र, जीवन से भरपूर लेकिन बेदाग।
एक्शन: क्रिएटर सब्जियां काट रही है, हरकतें कुशल और सटीक हैं (गैर-प्रदर्शन प्रकृति)।
विवरण: मैक्रो लेंस उस पल को कैप्चर करता है जब चाकू की धार सामग्री को काटती है, रस उछलता है। फिर मिट्टी के चूल्हे में झिलमिलाती नारंगी लौ पर स्विच होता है, प्रकाश और छाया गर्म और वास्तविक हैं।

[00:10-00:15] शॉट 3: शांत समय (पल)
दृश्य: फुल शॉट/मीडियम शॉट।
एक्शन: आंगन में लकड़ी की लंबी मेज पर एक नाजुक घर का बना व्यंजन रखा गया है। क्रिएटर चुपचाप बैठ जाती है, धीरे से एक बिखरे हुए बाल को ठीक करती है, और एक निवाला उठाती है।
वातावरण: बैकलाइट के खिलाफ भाप धीरे-धीरे ऊपर उठती है, दृश्य इतना शांत है कि आप लगभग हवा सुन सकते हैं, जो आधुनिक लोगों की परम विश्राम की लालसा को दर्शाता है।
```

### No. 4: डेमन स्लेयर लाइव-एक्शन बैटल प्रॉम्प्ट सीडेंस 2.0 के लिए

![中文](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-中文-red)
![Featured](https://img.shields.io/badge/⭐-Featured-gold)

#### 📖 विवरण

सीडेंस 2.0 के लिए एक विस्तृत, हाई-एनर्जी वीडियो प्रॉम्प्ट, जिसमें डेमन स्लेयर-शैली की लड़ाई (वॉटर ब्रीदिंग बनाम थंडर ब्रीदिंग) का 15-सेकंड का लाइव-एक्शन रूपांतरण तैयार किया गया है। प्रॉम्प्ट में शैली (हॉलीवुड लाइव-एक्शन मंगा रूपांतरण, डार्क समुराई, 4K, अत्यधिक क्विक कट, पार्टिकल लाइट इफेक्ट्स), सेटिंग (रात में धुंधला जंगल), और तीन अलग-अलग शॉट्स निर्दिष्ट किए गए हैं, जो पात्रों की गतिविधियों, पावर-अप अनुक्रमों और अंतिम टकराव का विवरण देते हैं।

#### 📝 प्रॉम्पट

```
लाइव-एक्शन मंगा रूपांतरण · ब्रीदिंग स्टाइल निर्णायक युद्ध (15 सेकंड · अल्ट्रा-बर्निंग स्पेशल इफेक्ट्स संस्करण)
【मुख्य फोकस】: वाटर ब्रीदिंग (ब्लू वाटर ड्रैगन) बनाम थंडर ब्रीदिंग (गोल्डन लाइटनिंग), लाइव-एक्शन अत्यधिक गति वाला द्वंद्व।
【शैली】: हॉलीवुड लाइव-एक्शन मंगा रूपांतरण फिल्म गुणवत्ता, डार्क समुराई शैली, 4K अल्ट्रा-क्लियर, अत्यधिक त्वरित कट, पार्टिकल लाइट इफेक्ट्स विस्फोट, कोई रक्तपात नहीं।
【अवधि】: 15 सेकंड
【दृश्य】: चांदनी रात में धुंधला जंगल, कीचड़ भरी जमीन, गिरते पत्ते।
[00:00-00:05] शॉट 1: वाटर मेलोडी प्रील्यूड · स्टार्टिंग फॉर्म (चार्जिंग का एहसास)
विजुअल्स: हरे और काले चेकर वाले हाओरी (जैकेट) पहने एक युवा समुराई, चांदनी में अपने गुरुत्वाकर्षण के केंद्र को नीचे करता है, दोनों हाथों से अपनी तलवार पकड़े हुए।
एक्शन: वह गहरी सांस लेता है, और आसपास की हवा तुरंत जम जाती है। जैसे ही वह अपनी तलवार निकालता है, उच्च दबाव वाले पानी के प्रवाह से संघनित एक विशाल नीला जल ड्रैगन, हवा से प्रकट होता है, उसके शरीर और ब्लेड के चारों ओर तेजी से घूमता है, बहते पानी की गर्जना उत्सर्जित करता है।
स्पेशल इफेक्ट्स विवरण: पानी के प्रवाह में यथार्थवादी छींटे का एहसास होता है, जो अंधेरे जंगल को रोशन करता है।
[00:05-00:10] शॉट 2: थंडर फ्लैश · एडवांस (अत्यधिक गति का एहसास)
विजुअल्स: उसके सामने वाला प्रतिद्वंद्वी, पीले त्रिकोणीय पैटर्न वाले हाओरी पहने एक गोरा तलवारबाज, अपने शरीर को बहुत नीचे झुकाता है, इयाइजुत्सु (तलवार खींचने की तकनीक) की मुद्रा लेता है।
एक्शन: जमीन अचानक फट जाती है। उसका पूरा शरीर तुरंत एक चमकदार सुनहरी बिजली के आफ्टरइमेज में बदल जाता है, जो नग्न आंखों को अदृश्य गति से पेड़ों के माध्यम से 'Z' आकार में तेजी से अपवर्तित और आगे बढ़ता है।
स्पेशल इफेक्ट्स विवरण: उसके गुजरने वाले स्थानों पर सुनहरी बिजली के आर्क और झुलसे हुए गिरे हुए पत्ते रह जाते हैं।
[00:10-00:15] शॉट 3: वाटर-थंडर टकराव · अंतिम ध्वनि (अंतिम चालों का टकराव)
विजुअल्स: अत्यधिक गति का आमने-सामने का टकराव। युवा समुराई हमले का सामना करने के लिए विशाल नीले जल ड्रैगन को नीचे घुमाता है, और बिजली में बदला हुआ गोरा तलवारबाज उसमें टकराता है।
एक्शन: फ्रेम के केंद्र में दोनों तलवारें हिंसक रूप से टकराती हैं।
स्पेशल इफेक्ट्स तमाशा: नीला जल ड्रैगन और सुनहरी बिजली तुरंत फट जाती है, जिससे एक विशाल जल-थंडर ऊर्जा तूफान बनता है जो बाहर की ओर फैलता है। आसपास के बड़े पेड़ ऊर्जा तरंग से आधे टूट जाते हैं, और कीचड़, पानी और प्रकाश कैमरे को अस्पष्ट कर देते हैं। दृश्य एक अत्यंत चमकदार नीली, पीली और सफेद रोशनी में समाप्त होता है।
```

### No. 5: Seedance 2.0: 80 वर्षीय रैपर MV

![中文](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-中文-red)
![Featured](https://img.shields.io/badge/⭐-Featured-gold)

#### 📖 विवरण

सीडेंस 2.0 के लिए एक विस्तृत, 15-सेकंड का प्रॉम्प्ट, जिसमें एक 80 वर्षीय महिला को दिखाते हुए 16:9 हॉरिजॉन्टल स्ट्रीट रैप म्यूजिक वीडियो (MV) जनरेट किया गया है। प्रॉम्प्ट में स्टाइल (नियॉन पर्पल/ब्लू कूल टोन, विस्फोटक माहौल), कैरेक्टर की उपस्थिति (सिल्वर बाल, लेदर जैकेट, हिप-हॉप एक्सेसरीज), सीन ब्रेकडाउन (0-3 सेकंड का इंट्रो, 3-7 सेकंड रैपिंग, 7-11 सेकंड डांसिंग, 11-15 सेकंड क्लाइमेक्स/निष्कर्ष), विशिष्ट रैप लिरिक्स, कैमरा तकनीक (लो एंगल, 360-डिग्री रोटेशन, क्विक कट्स), और साउंड डिज़ाइन (ट्रैप इलेक्ट्रॉनिक म्यूजिक, हैवी 808 ड्रम्स) निर्दिष्ट किए गए हैं।

#### 📝 प्रॉम्पट

```
16:9 हॉरिजॉन्टल स्क्रीन, स्ट्रीट रैप MV स्टाइल, नियॉन बैंगनी और नीले कूल टोन, विस्फोटक कूल और जोरदार माहौल। 0-3 सेकंड: मीडियम शॉट पुश-इन, शहर की सड़क का रात का दृश्य जिसमें नियॉन लाइटें चमक रही हैं, एक 80 वर्षीय चांदी के बालों वाली महिला एक भित्तिचित्र वाली दीवार के सामने खड़ी है, छोटे चांदी-सफेद बाल करीने से पीछे की ओर चिकने किए हुए हैं, विशिष्ट चौकोर चेहरे की रूपरेखा, तलवार जैसी भौहें कनपटी की ओर झुकी हुई हैं, आँखें बिजली की तरह तेज हैं, आँखों के कोनों पर झुर्रियाँ समय के बैज की तरह हैं, उसके मुँह के कोने पर एक आत्मविश्वास भरी मुस्कान है, एक सफेद प्रिंटेड टी-शर्ट (छाती पर बड़े काले अक्षर "YOLO") के ऊपर एक काली लेदर जैकेट + काली कार्गो पैंट + सफेद हाई-टॉप स्नीकर्स पहने हुए है, उसकी गर्दन में एक मोटी सोने की चेन का हार है, कलाई पर चांदी का कंगन है, दोनों हाथों से एक माइक्रोफोन पकड़े हुए है, BGM के जोरदार ड्रम बीट्स शुरू होते हैं, बूढ़ी महिला की आँखें तेज हो जाती हैं, और उसके होंठ रैप शुरू करने के लिए खुलते हैं। 3-7 सेकंड: मीडियम शॉट + क्लोज-अप स्विच, बूढ़ी महिला रैप करना शुरू करती है, जिसमें ताल की एक अत्यंत मजबूत भावना है, उसके चांदी के बाल उसके सिर हिलाने की हरकतों के साथ उड़ रहे हैं, एक हाथ में माइक्रोफोन पकड़े हुए है, दूसरा हाथ ताल से मेल खाने के लिए हावभाव बना रहा है—तर्जनी कैमरे की ओर इशारा कर रही है, हथेली ताल को ऊपर और नीचे काट रही है, हिप-हॉप हावभाव बना रही है, हरकतें सहज और प्रवाहमय हैं, आँखें तेज हैं और सीधे कैमरे की ओर देख रही हैं, झुर्रियाँ उसकी अभिव्यक्ति के साथ स्पष्ट रूप से उछल रही हैं, होंठ तेजी से खुल और बंद हो रहे हैं ताकि गीत निकल सकें: [रैप के बोल] "अस्सी साल की टाँगें, तुमसे बेहतर कूद सकती हैं! चांदी के बाल लहराते हुए, यह मेरा गर्व है! मुझे बूढ़ा मत कहो, मेरा फ्लो तुमसे बेहतर है, जब तुम रैप खेल रहे थे, मैं डिस्को सुन रही थी!" (तेज गति, मजबूत ताल, जोरदार रवैया) क्विक कट्स: चेहरे के क्लोज-अप, हाथ की हरकतें, पूरे शरीर का हिलना, साइड सिल्हूट, BGM बीट के साथ सिंक्रनाइज़। 7-11 सेकंड: डांस सेगमेंट, कैमरा पूरे शरीर को दिखाने के लिए पीछे हटता है, बूढ़ी महिला नाचना शुरू करती है—पहले क्लासिक हिप-हॉप बाउंस, फिर एक साफ स्ट्रीट डांस फ्रीज, उसके बाद कंधों से पैर की उंगलियों तक फैलने वाली बॉडी वेव, और फिर एक त्वरित फुटवर्क वर्कआउट, हरकतें साफ और तेज हैं, नियॉन लाइटों के नीचे चांदी के बाल उड़ते हैं, लेदर जैकेट हवा में फड़फड़ाती है, वह नाचते हुए रैप करना जारी रखती है: [रैप के बोल] "पैर और पंजे फुर्तीले हैं, गति धीमी नहीं है, मेरे बोल समय में खुदे हुए हैं! तुम फोन से खेलते हो, मैं बीट्स से खेलती हूँ, अस्सी साल का जीवन, इस छंद में लिखा गया है!" (तेज ताल, मजबूत टोन) लो-एंगल अपवर्ड शॉट + 360-डिग्री सराउंडिंग शॉट, बूढ़ी महिला के कूल और जोरदार डांस मूव्स को कैप्चर करते हुए। 11-15 सेकंड: चरमोत्कर्ष का अंत, बूढ़ी महिला एक कूल मोड़ लेती है, उसके चांदी के बाल हवा में एक चाप बनाते हैं, वह कैमरे की ओर मुड़ती है और अपनी उंगली से "शश" का इशारा करती है, फिर उसके होंठ माइक्रोफोन के करीब आते हैं, अंतिम पंक्ति को धीमी, चुंबकीय आवाज में गाते हुए: [वास्तविक गीत] "समय कभी किसी सुंदरता को नहीं हराता, मैंने बस जवानी का अनुभव करने का तरीका बदल दिया है..." (धीमी ताल, गहरी भावना, देर तक रहने वाला अंत) कैमरा धीरे-धीरे बूढ़ी महिला की आँखों के क्लोज-अप के लिए अंदर आता है, उसकी आँखों के कोनों पर झुर्रियाँ सभी कहानियाँ हैं, उसकी नज़र अभी भी तेज है फिर भी उसमें दयालुता का संकेत है, चरमोत्कर्ष पर BGM अचानक बंद हो जाता है, फ्रेम बूढ़ी महिला की कूल फिर भी थोड़ी कोमल मुस्कान पर जम जाता है, विग्नेटिंग + नियॉन बैंगनी प्रकाश प्रभामंडल।
```

### No. 6: सिनेमैटिक स्ट्रीट रेसिंग सीक्वेंस प्रॉम्प्ट

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)
![Featured](https://img.shields.io/badge/⭐-Featured-gold)

#### 📖 विवरण

Seedance 2.0 का उपयोग करके रात में सिनेमैटिक स्ट्रीट रेसिंग सीक्वेंस जनरेट करने के लिए एक विस्तृत प्रॉम्प्ट, जिसमें कैमरा मूवमेंट, शॉट टाइमिंग (0-12s), वातावरण का विवरण और वांछित विजुअल स्टाइल (Fast and Furious से प्रेरित) निर्दिष्ट है।

#### 📝 प्रॉम्पट

```
रात में सिनेमैटिक स्ट्रीट रेसिंग सीक्वेंस, एक हाई-परफॉर्मेंस कार के अंदर केंद्रित ड्राइवर स्टीयरिंग व्हील को पकड़े हुए, आंखों में तीव्र फोकस, विंडशील्ड पर शहर की रोशनी का प्रतिबिंब, अचानक एक्सीलरेशन से पहले बढ़ता तनाव

कैमरा: सीमलेस ट्रांजिशन के साथ रैपिड मल्टी-एंगल सिस्टम, इंटीरियर क्लोज़-अप → ओवर-द-शोल्डर → एक्सटीरियर ट्रैकिंग → लो ग्राउंड शॉट्स, अल्ट्रा डायनामिक कैमरा मूवमेंट, व्हिप पैन्स + स्पीड रैंप ट्रांजिशन + मोशन ब्लर मास्किंग कट्स, निरंतर प्रवाह का भ्रम

(0-2s) ड्राइवर का इंटीरियर क्लोज़-अप, हाथ गियर शिफ्ट पर कसता है, धीमी सांसें, डैशबोर्ड की लाइटें चमक रही हैं
(2-4s) ओवर-द-शोल्डर शॉट, सामने की सड़क नियॉन-लाइट वाले शहर में फैलती हुई, इंजन का कंपन बढ़ता हुआ
(4-6s) NOS बटन दबाती उंगली का एक्सट्रीम क्लोज़-अप, तुरंत इग्निशन रिएक्शन
(6-8s) विस्फोटक एक्सीलरेशन, कैमरा एक्सटीरियर साइड ट्रैकिंग शॉट पर स्नैप करता है, कार तेज गति के साथ आगे बढ़ती है
(8-10s) डामर के पास अल्ट्रा लो ग्राउंड शॉट, पहिए अत्यधिक गति से घूम रहे हैं, वातावरण तेजी से पीछे छूट रहा है
(10-12s) तंग गलियों में हाई-स्पीड चेज़, तीखे मोड़, कैमरा एंगल्स के बीच व्हिप पैन करता है, रिफ्लेक्शंस और लाइट ट्रेल्स गति को बढ़ाते हैं

घना शहरी रात का वातावरण, नियॉन लाइटों को प्रतिबिंबित करता गीला डामर, सुरंग के रास्ते, स्ट्रीट लाइटें, हाई-स्पीड शहर का माहौल
अल्ट्रा रियलिस्टिक, Fast and Furious से प्रेरित ऊर्जा, फोटो-रियलिस्टिक लाइटिंग, तीव्र मोशन ब्लर, हाई कंट्रास्ट नियॉन रिफ्लेक्शंस, सिनेमैटिक डेप्थ ऑफ फील्ड, गति का अत्यधिक अहसास, फ्लूइड ट्रांजिशन, कोई डिस्टॉर्शन नहीं, कोई स्ट्रेचिंग नहीं
```

### Hand-Painted Fantasy Animated Feature

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

> A storyboard-style prompt for creating a Japanese-inspired hand-painted fantasy animation with consistent characters and emotional storytelling.

#### 📝 प्रॉम्पट

```
IMPORTANT CHARACTER CONSISTENCY LOCK:

Use the provided character sheets as the ONLY reference.

Maintain the exact same:

faces
hairstyles
eye colors
outfits
accessories
proportions
animation style
color palette

Lyra, Kael, and all elves must look identical in every scene.

Do NOT redesign characters between shots.

The entire video must feel like one continuous animated feature film, not separate clips.

STYLE

Create a hand-painted fantasy animated movie inspired by the emotional storytelling of classic Japanese animation.

Visual style:

watercolor backgrounds
expressive character animation
soft cinematic lighting
magical realism
gentle camera movements
emotional facial expressions
smooth transitions between scenes
**SCENE 1 — "The World Beyond The Trees" (0:00-0:20)

Visual:

Aerial shot over an endless enchanted forest.

Ancient trees reach the sky.
Glowing flowers bloom.
Small magical spirits fly through the air.
The camera slowly moves toward the elf kingdom.
Lyra walks through the forest.

She touches an ancient tree.

The tree glows.

Lyra (softly):
 "The forest remembers everything... every soul that has ever walked beneath these trees."

She looks toward distant mountains.

Lyra:
 "But there is one world we have never understood... the world of humans."
**SCENE 2 — "The Boy Who Found Magic" (0:20-0:40)

Visual:

A quiet human village surrounded by mountains.
Kael walks alone carrying his sketchbook.

He draws landscapes.

He discovers a mysterious glowing seed inside ancient ruins.

He touches it.

The magic awakens.

Kael (whispering):
 "What are you?"

The seed projects a vision of the elf forest.

Kael looks amazed.

Kael:
 "A world hidden from everyone..."
**SCENE 3 — "The First Meeting" (0:40-1:00)

Visual:

Kael enters the elf kingdom.

The elves surround him.
Guardians hold their weapons.

Lyra steps forward.

Silence.

They look at each other.

A glowing leaf falls between them.

Elf Guardian:
 "A human cannot belong here."

Lyra looks at Kael.

Lyra:
 "Then why did the forest bring him?"

Kael shows the glowing seed.

Kael:
 "I didn't choose this place... it chose me."

Lyra reaches toward the seed.

The magic reacts.
**SCENE 4 — "Two Worlds, One Heart" (1:00-1:25)

Emotional Montage With Dialogue

Lyra teaches Kael about the forest.
Lyra:
 "Every tree has a story. Every river has a memory."

Kael smiles.

Kael:
 "Humans forget too easily... maybe that's why we need stories."
Scenes:

Walking through glowing forests

Sharing food during an elf festival

Drawing each other

Watching stars beside a magical lake

Flying above clouds

Lyra looks at the human world through Kael's drawings.
Lyra:
 "Your world is beautiful."

Kael smiles.

Kael:
 "Only because I finally have
```

### Cinematic Desert Survival Sequence

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

> A highly detailed, multi-scene prompt for a cinematic desert survival sequence involving vehicle dynamics, creature interaction, and aerial descent.

#### 📝 प्रॉम्पट

```
Create a 15 second cinematic desert survival sequence in 16:9 with grounded live action realism, natural body mechanics, accurate dust, convincing vehicle suspension, subtle film grain, restrained color grading, and seamless shot continuity. The setting is a sun bleached basin surrounded by dry brown mountains beneath a clear cyan sky. Sparse shrubs, cracked asphalt, faded yellow road lines, heat shimmer, and windblown grit make the landscape feel harsh and real. 0 to 3 seconds: Begin with a low centered wide shot from the middle of an empty highway using a 40 millimeter lens. A weathered blue camper van approaches from far distance, growing steadily larger without unnatural acceleration. Keep the camera nearly locked with faint wind vibration. Hard afternoon sunlight creates crisp shadows and warm windshield reflections. Cut inside the van for a brief front mounted shot. A tired middle aged woman drives while a bearded man watches the road, with two younger passengers and a scruffy cream dog visible among blankets, hanging utensils, maps, and handmade decorations. Use gentle road vibration, imperfect focus breathing, sunlight leaking through dusty glass, and believable nervous glances. 3 to 8 seconds: Cut to an extremely low roadside angle beside dormant alien creatures scattered across the asphalt. They resemble enormous desert arthropods built from cracked shell, leathery tissue, tangled rootlike fibers, dried mud, and embedded stones. The van passes behind them with realistic wheel motion and a pressure wave that stirs dust and loose fibers. Hold after the van exits. A small claw twitches in the foreground, then one massive creature slowly lifts its armored head. Its weight compresses its jointed legs, gravel shifts beneath it, dust falls from its body, and its wet mouth opens with restrained animal behavior. Make a subtle fearful camera push as the creature rises into a dominant low angle silhouette. 8 to 12 seconds: Hard cut to a distant elevated view across a grassy desert plain. A rugged male traveler descends beneath a huge black organic parachute shaped like four heavy flower petals. The canopy is stretched translucent membrane with irregular holes, scarred veins, and long elastic tendrils connected to his harness. Wind deforms it asymmetrically. His arms fight for balance, one foot is bare, the other wears a worn boot, and his clothing flaps with accurate drag. Use a long lens that compresses the mountains and makes the descent feel strangely graceful. 12 to 15 seconds: Shift into a fast lateral tracking shot near ground level as the traveler skims above shrubs, briefly disappearing behind a bush before reappearing. His legs bicycle in panic, his body swings naturally under tension, and his shadow races over uneven ground. He touches down awkwardly, stumbles two steps, then braces as the enormous black canopy collapses behind and around him
```

LOOK:
DV 16mm tape camcorder footage look. Soft, slightly blurry digital tape quality, faint tape noise, highlights that bloom subtly in bright daylight, gentle auto-exposure shifts as the camera moves between sunlight and shade, muted color contrast, realistic skin tones.

STYLE:
Relaxed daytime vlog with a calm, genuine, low-energy-but-happy vibe. Slightly tired after a long walk or outdoor workout, but satisfied and enjoying the fresh air. Handheld throughout, natural pauses between lines, occasional quiet breathing, authentic unposed expressions, documentary realism.

Character

JAY — a handsome Korean man in his 20s. Thick black hair slightly tousled from being outdoors, sharp yet warm Korean facial features, healthy glowing skin with a light sheen of sweat, expressive eyes, athletic slim build. Wearing a modest long-sleeve athletic top or lightweight hoodie, loose-fit joggers or athletic pants, comfortable sneakers. Fully covered arms and torso. No jewelry.

Setting

A peaceful public park during the daytime under natural sunlight. Wide green lawns, mature trees casting soft shadows, paved walking paths, park benches, trimmed bushes, birds occasionally flying overhead, gentle breeze moving the leaves, distant people walking or jogging naturally in the background without drawing attention, bright blue sky with soft clouds. Everything feels calm, open, and naturally lit.

Storyboard

(~2s, camera propped against a park bench, medium shot)
He walks into frame breathing slightly heavier from a walk or light workout, wipes his forehead with the back of his hand, gives a small relaxed smile.

JAY:
""Okay... just finished my walk, such a nice day.""

(~2s, handheld, slow pan across the park)
Camera slowly drifts across the green trees, walking paths, and open lawn before returning to him.

JAY (off-screen, softly):
""It's so peaceful here today.""

(~2s, medium handheld, beside a park bench)
He grabs his water bottle from the bench, takes a long drink, then exhales comfortably afterward.

JAY:
""Needed that so bad.""

(~1.5s, macro insert, shallow DOF)
Close-up on his hand wiping condensation from the water bottle, sunlight reflecting off tiny water droplets. No dialogue—only soft breathing and subtle ambient park sounds.

(~2s, medium handheld, open grassy area)
He props the camera against the bench, steps back into frame, casually stretches, then playfully does a few quick dance moves or light footwork before laughing
```

```
Main Subject: Young Korean woman, 23, natural everyday appearance, long black hair worn loose and slightly messy from the sea breeze, realistic skin texture with lightly sun-kissed cheeks, minimal makeup, cheerful and carefree personality. Wearing a loose white oversized linen shirt over a light blue tank top, beige linen shorts, white sandals, a woven straw hat, and a small canvas tote bag. Maintain consistent identity, clothing, hairstyle, and appearance throughout.

Location: A quiet beach on Jeju Island during an early summer morning. Soft golden sunlight, gentle ocean waves, black volcanic rocks, clear turquoise water, seashells scattered along the sand, a few distant fishermen, and a peaceful, uncrowded shoreline.

Visual Style: Ultra-realistic documentary realism. Genuine candid behavior, natural body language, authentic vacation atmosphere, warm morning light, rich environmental details.

Camera Style: Early-2000s Sony MiniDV camcorder. Heavy handheld shake, imperfect framing, autofocus hunting between the waves and her face, faded colours, soft contrast, slight motion blur, exposure pumping toward the bright ocean, MiniDV compression artifacts, no stabilization.

00:00–00:03
She walks barefoot along the shoreline carrying her sandals in one hand. Small waves wash over her feet as the camera briefly focuses on the water before finding her face.

00:03–00:06
She crouches to pick up a unique seashell, smiling as she brushes the sand away with her thumb. The operator moves closer, causing slight autofocus breathing.

Standing on a black volcanic rock, she watches the horizon while the sea breeze moves her hair and oversized shirt naturally.

She skips a flat stone across the calm water, laughing softly after it bounces several times. The camera shakes as the operator reacts a little too late.

00:12–00:15
She turns toward the camcorder with a warm smile, places the seashell inside her tote bag, and continues walking down the empty beach. The recording drifts toward the waves and abruptly cuts to black.

Audio: Natural ambience only–gentle waves, seabirds, light wind, distant fishermen talking, footsteps on wet sand, water splashing, and rustling fabric. No music. No narration.

Goal: A forgotten MiniDV home video from summer 2004, capturing a peaceful morning walk on Jeju Island that feels warm, imperfect, nostalgic, and completely authentic.

### Pixar Style Pigeon Chase Animation

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

> A long-form episodic video prompt for a Pixar-inspired 3D animated short film about a man and his pigeon nemesis.

#### 📝 प्रॉम्पट

```
MY NEMESIS IS A PIGEON” Style: Pixar-Inspired 3D Animation • Comedy • Family-Friendly • Viral Short Film ⸻ 🚪 ACT 1 — THE DAILY FEAR Dave is getting ready to leave his apartment. But something feels wrong. Before stepping outside, he carefully checks every direction. Left. Right. The sky. Behind a nearby tree. Even under a parked car. He’s clearly done this many times before. The audience immediately wonders: “What is this guy afraid of?” Dave finally relaxes. Maybe today will be different. ⸻ 🐦 ACT 2 — THE ENEMY RETURNS The moment Dave steps outside, everything changes. A shadow slowly moves across his face. He looks up. Perched high above him sits an angry pigeon. Not an ordinary pigeon. This pigeon looks like a movie villain. It stares directly at Dave. Waiting. Watching. The audience instantly understands: These two have history. ⸻ 🏃 ACT 3 — THE CHASE Without warning, the pigeon launches. Dave immediately panics. The chase begins. He runs through the city. Dodging pedestrians. Jumping around benches. Desperately trying to escape. The pigeon follows every move perfectly. No matter what Dave does, the pigeon always seems one step ahead. The audience starts laughing because the pigeon behaves less like a bird and more like a supervillain. ⸻ 🚌 ACT 4 — FALSE HOPE Dave spots a bus and jumps onboard. Finally safe. He collapses into a seat. Relieved. Then he hears a familiar sound. Coo. The pigeon is already inside the bus. Dave completely loses it. He screams for help. Begs the driver to stop. The bus screeches to a halt. Dave jumps out and runs. The audience realizes there is absolutely no escaping this bird. ⸻ 🌳 ACT 5 — THE PARK Dave runs into a nearby park. Exhausted. Breathing heavily. He hides behind a giant statue. Silence. No flapping. No cooing. Nothing. For the first time all day, he believes he has finally escaped. The danger seems over. ⸻ 🍟 ACT 6 — THE REVEAL A single french fry suddenly falls from the sky. Landing directly at Dave’s feet. His face drops instantly. He already knows what it means. Slowly, he looks up. The pigeon stands triumphantly on top of the statue. Then another french fry falls. As it drops— The story flashes back in black and white. Months earlier. A tiny baby pigeon excitedly approaches its favorite french fry. Just before it reaches the snack— Dave casually grabs it and eats it. The baby pigeon watches in absolute heartbreak. The audience finally discovers the truth. This entire feud started because of one stolen french fry. ⸻ 💩 ACT 7 — REVENGE COMPLETE Back in the present. Dave stares at the pigeon in disbelief. After all this time… The pigeon is still angry. The pigeon slowly nods. Then delivers one final perfectly aimed attack. Direct hit. Dave stands frozen in defeat. The pigeon proudly celebrates from atop the statue. Cut to black.
```

```
Create a 15-second ultra-realistic cinematic action scene in strict 16:9 landscape, native 4K, 24fps. Tone: explosive, dangerous, fast, high-tension, and visually thrilling. This is Scene 1 of a two-part boss-fight-style action sequence and must end on a cliffhanger that naturally leads into Scene 2. REFERENCE ROLES: image1 = Pyona, the main heroine. Use image1 as the strict reference for Pyona’s face, facial proportions, light pastel-blue bob hairstyle, hair color, white cloud hair clip, blue fish hair clip, body proportions, age impression, exact outfit, styling, and overall recognizable identity. Preserve her exact cropped urban-techwear action outfit throughout: light gray cropped hooded jacket, clearly visible white crop-top feel underneath, black asymmetrical utility mini-skirt / short-bottom styling, black utility pouch or crossbody detail, black socks, and black combat boots. Do not redesign her. image2 = the clown enemy. Use image2 as the strict reference for the clown’s identity, proportions, terrifying costume, giant heavy axe, and overall menacing appearance. Preserve the same clown and same weapon consistently throughout. CORE ACTION RULE: Pyona must never block the axe blade with her bare body. She survives and fights through prediction, timing, footwork, body-angle control, narrow dodges, aerial repositioning, spacing, and fast counters. The clown must feel physically overwhelming, heavy, and destructive. Pyona stays alive and gains control through speed and precision, not brute force. ENVIRONMENT: A ruined urban battlefield at night, like a collapsed industrial ruin or destroyed concrete structure. Broken walls, exposed rebar, cracked ground, rubble piles, dust clouds, shattered concrete, and damaged surfaces everywhere. The axe attacks must visibly damage the environment: scraping the ground, throwing sparks and debris, cracking walls, carving trenches, and blasting rubble outward. Maintain clear spatial consistency. STYLE: Live-action photorealism with strong action readability. High-end cinematic fight energy, like a boss fight in a dark action film. Realistic body weight, believable recoil, sharp contact, strong debris simulation, and dangerous near-miss tension. No comedy. No magical powers. No fantasy effects. CAMERA: Use dynamic but readable cinematic camera coverage: - low-angle handheld push-ins - side tracking shots - medium rotating follow shots - brief wide shots for scale and aftermath - one brief slow-motion emphasis only during the most dangerous near-miss. Cuts may be dynamic, but action must remain clear, coherent, and easy to follow. TIMELINE: 0.0–3.8s — Opening 3-hit pressure sequence Start immediately in motion with no pause. The clown launches an aggressive three-part attack sequence: 1. a huge horizontal axe sweep toward Pyona, 2. an immediate reverse backhand sweep, 3. a heavy downward slam. The first swing scrapes the ground and throws sparks, broken stone, and debris. Pyona performs a fast lateral slip-step dodge, narrowly avoiding the blade as it passes just beside her shoulder. Without pause, the clown reverses into the second sweep. Pyona ducks and twists her upper body under the returning blade, avoiding it by centimeters. The clown then commits into the third attack, a brutal overhead slam. Pyona instantly side-jumps or rolls diagonally away just before impact. The axe slams into the ground and carves a trench, blasting dust and rubble upward. The whole opening must feel relentless, like Pyona is under constant lethal pressure with no breathing room. 3.8–6.8s — Fast first counter / control disruption As the axe is briefly stuck or slowed from the heavy slam, Pyona instantly takes the opening. She leaps lightly off broken rubble or a damaged concrete edge and darts in with a short, sharp three-hit counter: - first, a quick strike to the clown’s weapon-control arm or axe-handle area, - second, another fast disrupting hit to the grip / forearm zone, - third, a clean straight punch to the clown’s chest. These hits are not about overpowering the clown, but about disrupting his balance and force generation. The clown stumbles backward and crashes against part of a ruined wall, creating visible cracks and shaking loose concrete dust. Pyona lands and immediately side-steps out again, never staying directly in front of the axe. 6.8–10.2s — Most dangerous near-miss The clown recovers faster than expected and instantly retaliates with a violent reverse horizontal axe sweep aimed directly at Pyona’s torso. This is the most dangerous beat of Scene 1. Use one brief slow-motion emphasis here. Pyona sharply twists her waist and slides half a step sideways at the very last moment. The axe blade misses her body by only a few centimeters, slicing past the side of her outfit and strongly disturbing the hem / edge of her cropped top and jacket without actually injuring her. Ground fragments and dust fly through frame. The near miss must feel terrifyingly close. The slow motion should be very brief, then snap back to full speed immediately. 10.2–12.8s — Clean momentum-based knockout counter Using the dodge momentum from the near miss, Pyona spins fluidly into a fast, powerful roundhouse kick. The kick lands cleanly and forcefully on the clown’s side face / jaw. The impact must feel sharp and real. The clown is blasted backward and crashes through part of a ruined wall or major concrete section. Broken concrete, rubble chunks, and a heavy dust burst explode across the frame. Pyona lands balanced and alert, not posing, already ready for the next move. 12.8–15.0s — Cliffhanger ending Wide aftermath shot. Dust and debris settle around the collapsed wall area. For a brief moment it seems Pyona has finally created space. Then movement appears inside the wreckage. The clown plants one hand into the rubble, grabs the giant axe, and forcefully rises again from the debris, still dangerous and not defeated. Pyona remains in the foreground or mid-ground in a guarded stance, breathing hard, watching him carefully and maintaining distance. End on the clown partially risen in the rubble with the axe in hand, clearly setting up Scene 2. AUDIO: No dialogue. Use only strong cinematic fight sound design: heavy axe swings, metal scrape on stone, ground carving, sparks, concrete cracking, rubble spray, debris fall, air displacement, fast footwork, body impact, wall collapse, dusty low-end impact booms, and tense dark action ambience. No spoken lines. IMPORTANT RULES: Pyona must never tank the axe blade. She must always evade first, then counter. The clown’s axe must feel extremely heavy and lethal. The clown’s attacks must come in chained pressure, not one isolated swing at a time. Pyona’s counters must be short, precise, and opening-based. Environmental damage must match the axe impacts. Keep both characters visually consistent with image1 and image2. Scene 1 must end with the clown rising again, not fully defeated. NEGATIVE: No direct body-blocking of the axe, no weak impact, no fake contact, no floating, no teleporting, no wire-fu, no random extra enemies, no comedy, no magical powers, no repeated identical attack loops, no unreadable blurry action, no identity drift, no hairstyle change, no outfit redesign, no loss of the visible crop-top feel in Pyona’s outfit, no soft or low-energy choreography.

> A complex multi-part video prompt for Seedance that blends live-action beach BBQ footage with a 2D anime sticker character, including detailed instructions for character consistency and interaction.

#### 📝 प्रॉम्पट

```
[Seedance 2.5 / 15 seconds / 9:16]

Use the reference image as the sole character reference. 15 seconds, 9:16 vertical, one cut, fixed camera. The camera is completely fixed from start to finish. Zooming, panning, tilting, rotating, handheld movement, cuts, transitions, reframing, slow motion, and freeze-frame effects are prohibited.

[Composition/Positioning]
Place a live-action BBQ grill and meat on the bone in the left 45% of the screen. Place a 2D character in the right 30% of the screen. Reserve about 20% of space in the center for tongs and moving the meat.

The character stands on the sandy beach on the right, always displaying their whole body from head to black flip-flops. Do not place the character in front of the grill, on top of the grill legs, or on the table. Leave clear space between the character and the grill, maintaining their standing position from start to finish.

Do not overlap the grill, meat, human hands, tongs, or gochujang bottle with the character's body. The character must not hide the meat or grill. Human hands or tongs must not unnaturally cross the character's face or body. Accurately maintain depth and foreground-background relationships between objects.

[Visual Style]
A comical video combining live-action BBQ footage at a beautiful midsummer seaside with a flat 2D animated sticker character.

Draw a blue sea, white waves, bright blue sky, golden sand, a distant coastline, and palm leaves swaying in the wind. Bright daytime natural light. Palm leaves, grill smoke, and the hem of the character's white shirt sway naturally in the sea breeze.

The background, sea, sand, grill, charcoal fire, smoke, flame, meat on the bone, fat, meat juice, gochujang, bottle, human hand, and metal tongs are all high-quality live-action expressions. Blown-out highlights, excessive bloom, haze, and exposure flickering are prohibited.

Only the character remains a flat 2D animated sticker until the end. Live-action, semi-live-action, 3D, CG figure, game model, Pixar style, or clay animation styles are prohibited. Maintain clear line drawings, cell shading, flat shadows, and thin white borders.

[Character Persistence]
Use the male character from the reference image exactly as they are. Fix the soft ash-silver wavy hair, bangs shape, hair length and volume, grey-blue eyes, young well-formed face, and slim long-limbed body type.

Fix the white sleeveless innerwear, sheer white short-sleeved open shirt, white drawcord, black paisley shorts, black flip-flops, silver necklace, earrings, bracelet, and ring.

Do not change the face, hairstyle, body type, clothing, patterns, colors, shoes, or accessories. Do not add hats, sunglasses, aprons, gloves, or new props. Do not duplicate the character. Do not add another 2D character.

The character is not coldly expressionless but moves very cutely and expressively. Clearly show large shining eyes, rosy cheeks, a happy smile, tilting the head, small shoulder-shaking movements, a sulking face with puffed cheeks, teary eyes, and frantic waving of hands. No standing stiffly.

[BBQ and Meat on the Bone]
Grill a large Tomahawk steak on the bone in the live-action BBQ grill on the left side of the screen. Long bones are clearly visible and physically connected to the meat. The meat is well-grilled, showing deep sear marks, savory char, glossy juices, melted fat, and light steam.

Do not make the meat raw, charred black, boneless, or deformed. Do not duplicate meat or bones. Do not let bones disappear midway.

A live-action adult woman's hand enters from the left edge of the screen. Maintain pink nail art, thin silver rings, and normal five fingers. Turn and lift the meat with metal BBQ tongs. Do not fuse the hand, fingers, ring, tongs, meat, or grill.

[Gochujang]
Use a transparent squeeze bottle with no label, text, or brand logo. The content is thick, glossy, deep reddish-brown gochujang. Do not make it bright red like ketchup or a watery liquid.

The gochujang flows in a thick continuous stream from the bottle nozzle and falls directly onto the meat surface. Do not make the sauce appear suddenly from mid-air. Do not leak from the bottom or sides of the bottle.

[00:00–00:03]
Start with a fixed wide composition. On the left, a Tomahawk steak on the bone is sizzling loudly. A woman's live-action hand slowly turns the meat once with metal tongs. Fat falls onto the charcoal, and a small flame rises for a moment. Thin smoke drifts in the sea breeze.

The 2D character on the right stares at the meat, their eyes sparkling large. They put their hands together under their chin, raise their shoulders, bend their knees slightly, and bounce twice. A small 2D drop of drool appears at the mouth for a moment and disappears.

[00:03–00:05]
The character finds the gochujang bottle. Their eyes sparkle, they tilt their head, and point at the bottle with one hand. They happily hold the bottle with both hands and hug it to their chest for a moment.

Then, with a mischievous smile and one eye closed, they point the nozzle toward the meat on the left. The character themselves does not move from their standing position on the right.

[00:05–00:07]
The character squeezes the bottle hard, pouring a large amount of gochujang onto the meat on the bone. A thick reddish-brown sauce extends continuously from the nozzle to the meat, creating a large zigzag pattern on the meat surface.

The sauce bubbles lightly with the heat and mixes with the meat juice to create a gloss. Maintain visibility of the bone and grill marks without completely hiding the whole meat.

The character smiles proudly, sticks out their tongue slightly, and shakes their shoulders cutely while squeezing the bottle. The woman's hand stops in surprise while holding the tongs.

[00:07–00:09]
The woman's hand quickly takes the gochujang bottle with the tongs and returns it to the left. Immediately after, she lightly taps the top of the character's head once with the tip of the closed tongs.

The tip of the tongs clearly and lightly touches the character's hair. At the moment of contact, a small yellow 2D star-shaped impact effect appears over the head. Do not hit hard. Do not make it painful. Make it a completely cute cartoon-style reprimand.

The moment they are hit, the character's head tilts to the right, their silver hair bounces, and their shoulders and torso shake greatly to the left and right. Knees bend, and they spread their arms to balance. Feet do not leave the sand, and they do not move toward the grill.

They puff their cheeks, close their eyes tight, and shed a single small tear. Clearly show the cute shaking movement of the whole body, not just the head, for about 1 second.

[00:09–00:11]
The woman's hand returns the tongs to the grill and lifts a bite-sized piece of grilled meat with gochujang on it. The lifted meat shows grill marks, juice, fibers, and thick gochujang.

The tongs pass through the center space and carry the meat to the front of the character's mouth. The character sulks about being hit on the head, crosses their arms, turns their face away, and puffs their cheeks.

Immediately they glance at the meat with just their eyes. Their eyes sparkle again, they uncross their arms, put both hands behind their back, and lean their upper body slightly forward. They open their mouth wide and approach the meat.

[00:11–00:12]
The character clearly [eats] the meat

### Cinematic One-Shot Beach to Space Video Loop

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

> A complex 30-second seamless one-shot video prompt that transitions through multiple environments including a beach at sunset, an underwater scene, a New York City street, and outer space, eventually looping back to the start.

#### 📝 प्रॉम्पट

```
A breathtaking, high-quality, ultra-photorealistic cinematic 30-second single-shot video with smooth, continuous camera work and no cuts. The young Japanese woman from Image 1, with her signature long dark hair, wispy bangs, serene face, and warm smile, wearing an elegant white summer dress with a blue floral pattern, is the main character throughout. [0-5s] Japan Beach Golden Hour: The young woman is walking gracefully along the sandy shore of Shonan Beach during a glorious golden hour sunset. Warm, soft rim light glows on her hair and shoulders. She looks back over her right shoulder directly toward the camera, smiling warmly. The camera slowly orbits 360 degrees around her clockwise. [5-10s] Hawaii Beach Walk: As the 360-degree rotation completes, the scenery seamlessly transitions into a tropical Hawaiian beach with turquoise waters. Her facial identity, hairstyle, and smile remain perfectly consistent. She continues walking into the gentle waves of the ocean. The camera follows her, submerging smoothly under the clear water with natural motion blur. [10-15s] Majestic Undersea: Beautiful sun rays pierce through the water surface. The young woman glides gracefully and peacefully through the crystal-clear deep ocean water. Her hair and dress flow naturally with the gentle currents. Colorful tropical fish swim around her. A small vibrant fish swims upwards, and the camera follows it, rising rapidly to the surface. [15-20s] New York City: Breaking through the water surface, the scene seamlessly transforms into a bustling street in Manhattan, New York, during sunset. She continues walking elegantly along the sidewalk, her facial identity and serene expression perfectly consistent. A chic, modern light coat naturally appears over her dress. The camera tracks her smoothly as she walks toward a grand glass-walled skyscraper. [20-25s] Building Interior to Rooftop: The camera seamlessly enters the building through the glass, moving past a modern office lobby. She walks through the corridor and steps onto a spacious high-rise rooftop helipad. The camera slowly orbits her and begins to ascend vertically, revealing the beautiful city skyline scaling down below under a purple sunset sky. [25-30s] Space to Shonan (Perfect Loop): The camera passes through the soft clouds into the high atmosphere and outer space, showing a beautiful panoramic view of the Earth. The young woman floats weightlessly and peacefully in space. The camera rotates 180 degrees around her and dives gracefully back down through the clouds. A splash of water clears, returning to the original Shonan Beach. She stands in the exact same position, looking back and smiling warmly at the camera, forming a perfect, seamless loop.
```

### Poetic Xianxia Visual Illusion Comedy Video

![中文](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-中文-red)

> A detailed cinematic multi-shot video prompt for Seedance 2.0 featuring a poetic Xianxia-style comedy with visual illusions inspired by Buster Keaton.

#### 📝 प्रॉम्पट

```
[Generation Mode]

Seedance 2.0 Fast

[Video Specifications]

Strictly generate a 15-second video, 16:9 aspect ratio, with three clear shots.

[Overall Style]

Poetic Xianxia visual illusion comedy, realistic cinematic high-definition texture. Uses classical Chinese negative space composition, restrained deadpan performance, soft morning light, low-saturation jade green and ivory white tones, and fine film grain. Borrows from Buster Keaton-style spatial illusions, revealing the truth through controlled camera rotation rather than relying on failed magic for laughs. The entire film is set in a pure ancient Chinese world with no modern elements.

[Character Locking]

Use @Image 1 and @Image 2 to strictly lock character identity, face, hairstyle, body proportions, clothing, shoes, and accessories.

Character ID A | Senior Sister Sword Immortal

An East Asian female aged 25-30, oval face, natural fair skin tone, dark almond eyes, black long hair half-tied and fixed with a white jade hairpin, tall and slender build. Wearing white embroidered silk Hanfu with semi-transparent layered wide sleeves, silver waist seal, jade pendant, and white cloth boots.

Character ID B | Junior Sister

An East Asian female aged 20-25, rounded and agile face, black hair in braids, petite build. Wearing bluish-green linen Hanfu, dark belt, wooden hairpin, and black cloth shoes.

[Shot 1 | 0-5s | Wide Shot or Long Shot]

The frame is intentionally rotated 90 degrees clockwise, making a steep mountain wall appear horizontally as the ground. 

Character ID A: Senior Sister, appearing in full from head to toe, seems to be walking upright and calmly along the vertical cliff with hands behind her back, while her hair and sleeves naturally hang toward the vast abyss in the frame.

Character ID B: Junior Sister seems to be hanging onto an ancient pine tree below.

[Shot 2 | 5-10s | Medium Shot or Cowboy Shot]

The same Junior Sister in green linen Hanfu looks up and asks: "Senior Sister, is this 'Treading the Wall to Heaven'?"

The same Senior Sister in white silk Hanfu continues walking calmly and replies indifferently: "If the heart is upright, heaven and earth will naturally be level."

The background remains the same mountain wall, pine tree, floating mist, stone platform, and distant roofed water pavilion; the incorrect vertical orientation must be maintained without altering character anatomy.

[Shot 3 | 10-15s | Close-up or Extreme Close-up]

The camera smoothly rotates 90 degrees counter-clockwise to restore the true horizontal line, revealing that the Senior Sister is simply walking on a flat stone platform next to a low wall, while the Junior Sister is just casually leaning against the pine tree.

Junior Sister says expressionlessly: "Senior Sister, the world is level, but the camera is crooked."

Extreme Close-up: The Senior Sister's hard-maintained dignified smile instantly freezes, one eyebrow twitches slightly, and she then replies as if nothing happened: "This is to test your eyesight."

Ends with a dry pluck of a guqin string and the sound of a pine cone falling onto the stone slab.

[Technical Requirements]

Strict total duration of 15 seconds, 16:9 aspect ratio, three clear shots, original Mandarin dialogue, stable character faces and clothing, continuous scene geometry. Physics of mist, silk fabric, hair, pine trees, and gravity must be realistic and consistent. Do not generate on-screen subtitles. Only two visible characters should appear throughout.

[Negative Prompts]

blurry, bad quality, low quality, low resolution, noisy, jpeg artifacts, watermark, text, error; deformed, mutated, bad anatomy, poorly drawn hands, bad composition, out of frame, disfigured; inconsistent character, changing clothes, face morphing, background shift, glitching cuts, disappearing props
```

> A photorealistic handheld phone-style montage showing a woman repotting a houseplant with natural sound and lighting.

#### 📝 प्रॉम्पट

```
Handheld home-video style montage, 7 continuous shots with varied angles (avoid a single camera angle or one-take). Filmed one-handed on a phone with slight handheld shake, natural outdoor daylight, subtle film grain, photorealistic realism.

A woman Image1 repots a houseplant alone on a cozy apartment balcony. Image1 is used only for her face and hairstyle. She wears a faded denim short-sleeve shirt over a white tank top with a dirt-smudged canvas gardening apron. The narrow balcony has potted plants, terracotta pots, potting soil, gardening tools, string lights, and warm midday sunlight. She is the only person in the video. The plant goes from being root-bound in a cracked plastic pot to freshly repotted and watered. Dialogue is natural spoken Korean.

Shot 1 (0–2s): She taps and squeezes the old plastic pot to loosen the plant. "자, 나와라~"

Shot 2 (2–4s): Close overhead as she removes the plant, exposing tightly coiled roots and gently loosening them.

Shot 3 (4–6s): She places it into a terracotta pot, fills it with fresh soil by hand, and says, "훨씬 낫다."

Shot 4 (6–8s): She brushes dirt from the leaves, straightens the plant, and says, "There we go."

Shot 5 (8–10s): She wipes her dirty hands on her apron and smiles with a small approving nod.

Shot 6 (10–13s): She waters the plant with a small metal watering can. Soil darkens as it absorbs the water. "쭉쭉 마셔."

Shot 7 (13–15s): She steps back, leans on the balcony railing, admires the plant, wipes her brow, and exhales contentedly.

Audio: No music—only natural sounds of tapping plastic, crumbling soil, rustling roots, patting soil, fabric movement, pouring water, light breeze, birds, and a quiet satisfied breath.

No subtitles, text, logos, or watermarks. Do not recreate or copy the reference image itself.
```

### Astronaut to Apartment Match Cut Transition

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

> A sophisticated 15-second video prompt featuring a continuous motion match cut from an astronaut in space to a person in a VR headset.

#### 📝 प्रॉम्पट

```
FORMAT 15s / free rhythm / 1 MATCH CUT / CONTINUOUS MOVE UNTIL MATCH CUT + IMMEDIATE ACTION FROM FIRST FRAME

SUBJECTS: A lone astronaut in a damaged EVA suit desperately repairs a failing spacecraft while floating above Earth. The same person is later revealed sitting in a small apartment wearing casual clothes, where a VR headset appears only after the match cut and is removed in one uninterrupted motion.

ENVIRONMENT: Outer space above Earth’s glowing blue atmosphere, drifting debris, blinking warning lights, frozen silence broken by suit alarms. Transition into a dim but cozy apartment filled with scattered electronics, coffee mugs, and soft morning light spilling through curtains.

MOOD: High-stakes survival dissolves into everyday reality without breaking movement or spatial continuity.

COLOR LOGIC: Naturalistic Film Print Emulation.

⸻

TIMELINE

0:00–0:07

One continuous handheld-style zero-gravity camera move, WS gradually collapsing into MCU. The astronaut tumbles toward a damaged satellite panel as warning lights flash across the visor. One gloved hand desperately reaches for a sparking cable while the other stabilizes against the hull. Loose debris slowly spins past the lens. The Earth fills half the background, slowly rotating beneath. The camera floats with the subject, maintaining believable inertia and subtle drift while slowly shifting from a 24mm to 35mm perspective.

SFX: (oxygen warning beeps, suit breathing, metallic creaks, distant electrical crackle, gloved impacts against metal, radio static)

Cold sunlight creates sharp highlights across the visor while Earth’s blue glow softly fills the shadows.

⸻

0:07–0:11

Without cutting, the camera tightens into a dead-on CU. Sparks burst inches from the astronaut’s helmet as a damaged panel begins separating from the spacecraft. Just before impact, a woman’s voice calmly calls,

“Alex…”

Then louder,

“ALEX!”

The astronaut exhales in annoyance instead of panic.

Everything suddenly enters slow motion.

Floating debris nearly freezes.

The separating panel hangs motionless.

Only the astronaut continues moving at normal speed.

The camera slowly orbits clockwise around the helmet while maintaining identical framing and distance.

Instead of reaching for the failing equipment, both hands rise toward the helmet in one smooth, deliberate motion.

No headset or gaming device is visible anywhere in space.

Maintain identical face size, hand position, lens distance, head angle, and clockwise motion until the match cut.

SFX: (radio distortion stretching into silence, slowed warning alarms, breathing, woman’s voice echoing “Alex… ALEX…”, ambient hum fading)

⸻

0:11–0:15

MATCH CUT.

CU to MS.

The rising hands occupy the exact same screen position as the helmet instantly becomes a VR headset in a modest apartment.

The motion never stops.

For the first time, the VR headset is visible.

The subject grips both sides and smoothly lifts
```

CAMERA Grounded travel-documentary cinematography.  Mix: wide environmental coverage medium handheld following shots brief close reaction shots  Natural handheld instability. Time suddenly accelerates again and the cards begin falling rapidly around him. Without looking away from the camera, he reaches into the collapsing cloud of cards and cleanly catches a single card between two fingers. All remaining cards fall onto the table. Close-up: he slowly turns the captured card toward the camera—the seven of clubs. Faintest smile. Cut to black. Jazz fades into AND EDITING RULES: Each action must be fully completed before the next cut. Use clean hard cuts only. No dissolves, no morphing transitions, no flash transitions, no AI-style warping, and no continuous blending between different actions. Every new shot begins with the hands and deck already in the correct starting pose for that action. Do not visibly transform one grip into another on screen. AUDIO: It should remain subtle and never overpower the card sounds. Every action must have synchronized, realistic card audio: friction, sliding, snapping, dropping, spreading, flipping, flying, and not generate background music for the entire video except for SFX. VISUAL STYLE: Ultra-realistic live-action cinema. Real actor, real casino environment, real card material, real hand structure, and physically believable movement. Skin must show natural pores, slight tonal unevenness, knuckle creases, and realistic tension in the fingers. Cards must have believable thickness, slight edge wear, realistic flexibility, and consistent physical inertia. Use restrained exposure, soft highlight roll-off, deep layered shadows, realistic focus transitions, subtle sensor noise, and natural motion blur.

Japanese-inspired psychedelic abstract animation Core Style: Fast, vivid, unstable. Fully hand-drawn with rich organic textures. Every transition is fluid and natural—never rigid or mechanically cut. The entire frame, including the background, remains in constant motion, driven simultaneously by emotion, gravity, and heat. Every frame should feel alive, unpredictable, and physically reactive. [0–7.5s: Origin and Chain Reaction] [0–1.5s] A central abstract figure erupts into violent motion. Its emotional color core radiates outward as irregular pulsating waves while its silhouette tears apart in multiple directions. The background bends around the figure like compressed soft paper. Fine hand-drawn textures crawl rapidly across the entire frame, moving significantly faster than the figure itself, creating intense tension between body, skin, and surface. [1.5–3s] The separated contours accelerate into nervous linear forms. The figure's colorful core stretches into sharp ribbons and liquid stains. Some ribbons snap backward while others whip violently forward, leaving trembling trails behind. Empty spaces between movements ignite into new forms that emerge to fill emotional voids. [3–4.5s] Every shape surges diagonally across the composition. One massive form folds like fabric, revealing layered textures of grain, chalk, wet ink, scratched paint, and rough pigment beneath its surface. The background folds together with it, twisting into entirely new color fields. Smaller forms are dragged into constantly evolving speed lines. [4.5–6s] The image fractures into four emotional forces: calm, violence, vulnerability, and ecstasy. Each absorbs color from the others. Shadows appear before their objects, behaving like prophecies. The original forms chase these shadows, but the shadows mutate faster—becoming brighter, thicker, and more dominant until they surpass the objects themselves. [6–7.5s] A rapid chain reaction begins. Colliding forms generate visual motifs derived from the reference imagery: fragments, symbols, silhouettes, stains, and expressive linework. These newly born motifs accelerate in different directions. Frozen traces solidify into new entities before melting again and splitting into tiny emotional echoes. --- [7.5–15s: Compression and Extreme Transformation] [7.5–9s] The entire frame collapses toward an invisible point beneath the center. Every visual layer compresses inward at a different speed. Maximum compression lasts for only one frame before exploding upward and outward with vertical smears, shaking outlines, and exaggerated overshoot motion. Every element vibrates before settling into a completely new position. [9–10.5s] Textures migrate between forms. Dry rough textures sharpen previously fluid shapes into angular structures. Liquid textures soften rigid forms until they sag and drip. Luminous colorful textures dissolve into the background, causing the environment itself to pulse rhythmically. Every object changes behavior according to the texture it inherits. [10.5–12s] A massive rotational force sweeps across the frame. Every form flips, inverts, or turns inside out, exposing hidden reverse sides possessing qualities of hands, hooks, flames, or waves. Every collision triggers escalating chain reactions, transforming the entire visual world into an entirely new emotional reality. [12–13.5s] All boundaries dissolve. Forms merge, repel, and merge again, generating hybrid entities that inherit the colors and movement of their predecessors while becoming completely different beings. The background pushes forward into the foreground, becoming part of the forms themselves while releasing new shapes from within. [13.5–15s] Every previous process returns simultaneously at maximum intensity: contour separation, color bleeding, texture migration, and shadow-leading motion. Every frame is densely packed with intentional visual impact, fully absorbing and reinterpreting every reference element introduced earlier. --- [15–22.5s: Fragmentation and Neurotic Storm] [15–16.5s] The storm collides with an invisible barrier. The center explodes into countless shards of water-covered broken glass. Within each fragment, glowing spiral irises rotate violently, generating centrifugal force that stretches and tears at the shard edges. Neon magenta and electric blue leak through every crack. [16.5–18s] The fragments fuse into overlapping collaged human faces. The faces are composed of purple stains, scratched paint, and rough pigment. Their expressions oscillate continuously between terror and ecstasy. Jagged lightning-like lines pierce through them like exposed nerve fibers, violently stitching the faces together. [18–19.5s] Color saturation reaches its peak. Electric blue lines solidify into physical cables stretching throughout space, twitching uncontrollably while releasing neon magenta sparks. The compressed paper background burns away, transforming into a charred, granular carbon-black surface. [19.5–21s] Gravity reverses. Liquid elements such as water droplets and wet ink surge upward while broken glass continues falling downward, producing intense visual tearing. Vertical smears appear along the edges of the frame as though an unseen force is physically wiping the entire animation off the canvas. [21–22.5s] Every form is pulled into countless miniature spiral-iris black holes. The irises rapidly expand until they dominate the entire screen, spinning at an increasingly suffocating speed. The rough tactile quality of chalk and dry brush textures contrasts violently against the brilliant electrical center, creating overwhelming sensory overload. --- [22.5–30s: Aftershock and Eternal Echo] [22.5–24s] The giant iris evolves into pulsating biological tissue. The image breathes with a neurotic rhythm. Hidden symbols and textures appear and disappear beneath muscular contractions. Paint seepage creates convincing depth, as though layered across multiple transparent sheets of film. [24–25.5s] The animation enters an intense high-frequency vibration. Every object freezes in place while its edges oscillate violently like strings stretched to their breaking point. Violet shadows crawl across every surface, attempting to consume the remaining light. [25.5–27s] Rainbow-colored glass fragments and water droplets sweep across the frame like rain, refracting and distorting everything behind them through lens-like optical effects. Colors become heavy fluid masses that sink downward, leaving behind anxious, restless linear marks. [27–28.5s] The entire composition collapses into a single glowing horizontal jagged line. The background becomes a dark neon field filled with coarse grain. The line contains all accumulated energy, twisting violently while alternating flashes of electric blue and neon magenta pulse across it. [28.5–30s] The glowing line disintegrates into hand-drawn dust particles that drift rhythmically across the screen before gradually reassembling into one enormous blurred luminous silhouette. The final frame ends with tiny blocks of color continuing to vibrate subtly, leaving behind an everlasting emotional aftershock.

Seedance 2.0 Fast

Strictly generate a 15-second video, 16:9 landscape, with three clean and clear shots.

Movie-level realistic texture, using classic adventure film-style visual misdirection, silent film-style deadpan rhythm, stormy coastal atmosphere, restrained character performance, clear foreground and background spatial scheduling, and scale revelation driven by camera movement; comedy comes from two characters handling a harmless object with an overly serious heroic posture, with a second visual reversal added at the end.

Character ID A | Sword Immortal Female

Use @Image 1 as strict identity reference for Character ID A: the same 25–30 year old East Asian Sword Immortal female, oval face, natural fair skin, sharp dark brown eyes, black long straight hair, tall and slender figure, wearing white cloth boots, flowing white embroidered silk Hanfu, semi-transparent layered wide sleeves, silver waist ornaments, wearing a jade hairpin and carrying a silver long sword.

Character ID B | Cycling Female

[Core Props]

The same silver long sword, the same bicycle, a red long rope, a giant inflatable dragon kite, and a larger pink pig kite.

[Shot 1 | 0-5s | Low Angle Wide Shot]

A seaside rooftop in the evening wind, with wet concrete ground, metal railings, wind-torn warning tape, distant tall buildings, black clouds, sea fog, and fierce gusts establishing the environment.

A huge black flying dragon silhouette struggles wildly above the city skyline, lightning illuminating its spread wings, accompanied by low roars.

Character A enters the frame fully from head to toe, drawing the silver long sword; Character B steadies her bicycle beside her, both looking up with solemn expressions.

[Shot 2 | 5-10s | Medium Side Tracking Shot]

The same Sword Immortal female in white embroidered silk Hanfu throws the same silver long sword into the air, while the same cycling female in the yellow windbreaker pedals the same bicycle quickly, tightening a red rope connected to the "dragon."

[Shot 3 | 10-15s | Reveal Close-up to Extreme Close-up]

A child's voice comes from off-camera: "Auntie, mine is the pink pig!"

The two turn their heads slowly.

Focus shifts behind them, where an even larger pink pig kite is drifting further away with the wind.

The dragon must feel extremely heavy, ancient and physically realistic. MASTER RIDER LOCK: One adult armoured rider is already seated securely in a dark leather saddle on the dragon from the very first frame. The rider holds leather reins connected to the saddle and remains seated throughout the entire sequence. The rider does not suddenly appear, mount, dismount or change clothing. During the first shot, the rider is naturally hidden behind the dragon’s large head, neck and folded wings. ENVIRONMENT AND LIGHTING: The sequence takes place inside a gigantic ancient mountain cave. The cave contains rough black rock walls, enormous stone formations, drifting mist, scattered bones and faint glowing embers between cracks in the ground. The far interior is almost black, but the dragon must remain readable through soft amber reflections and cold blue rim light entering from the cave mouth. The cave entrance opens from a high mountain cliff into a vast dramatic landscape of jagged mountains, deep valleys, distant waterfalls, rolling clouds and pale stormy daylight. Maintain a cinematic cold-blue and muted-amber colour palette with deep shadows, volumetric light rays and realistic atmospheric haze. Dark and mysterious, but never so underexposed that the dragon or rider becomes blurry or invisible. SHOT 1 — DRAGON EMERGES, 0–5 SECONDS: Begin with a symmetrical low frontal wide shot positioned near the cave entrance, facing deep into the darkness. The camera slowly tracks backward toward the entrance as the enormous dragon walks forward out of the black cave interior. The dragon’s wings remain completely folded against both sides of its body. Its glowing amber eyes appear first, followed by its horned head, shoulders and powerful forelegs becoming visible through the mist. Each heavy footstep shakes the cave floor and dislodges small stones and dust from the ceiling. The dragon exhales dense vapour from its nostrils and releases a deep restrained growl. SHOT 2 — RIDER REVEAL, 5–9 SECONDS: Clean hard cut to a low three-quarter side tracking shot beside the moving dragon. Begin close to the dragon’s horned head and sharply detailed scales. Track smoothly backward along the dragon’s neck and folded wing while the dragon continues walking toward the cave entrance. As the camera reaches the dragon’s shoulder, reveal the single armoured rider seated in the saddle between the folded wings. Hold the composition long enough to clearly show the rider’s silver armour, helmet, dark cloak and both hands gripping the reins. The rider looks toward the bright cave exit and leans slightly forward in preparation for takeoff. The dragon and rider remain in continuous forward motion. Do not show a separate rider anywhere else in the cave. SHOT 3 — REAR TAKEOFF AND FLIGHT, 9–15 SECONDS: Clean hard cut to an epic wide rear view positioned behind and slightly above the dragon and rider. The bright cave mouth and vast mountain landscape fill the background ahead of them. The dragon accelerates into a powerful run toward the cave opening while its wings remain folded enough to pass safely through the cave. The dragon reaches the cliff edge and makes one forceful leap completely out of the cave. Only after clearing the rock opening, it spreads both enormous wings to their full symmetrical width. The sudden wing expansion pushes mist, dust and loose stones outward from the cave entrance. The rider leans forward, grips the reins and remains firmly attached to the saddle. The dragon performs one enormous downward wingbeat, catches the air and transitions naturally into stable forward flight. Loose stones fall from the cliff beneath it. The camera rapidly follows from behind as the dragon and rider soar away over the deep valley between jagged mountain peaks. The long tail streams naturally behind the dragon and the rider’s cloak whips in the wind. The wings remain folded inside the cave and open only after the dragon has completely cleared the cave mouth. The wings must not clip through the cave walls. The rider must not float above the saddle or merge into the dragon. No fire breathing, combat, dialogue, text, logos, blood or visible injuries.

```
कैमरा / लुक: विषय द्वारा स्वयं फिल्माया गया हैंडहेल्ड मिनी DV कैमकोर्डर फुटेज। हल्का हाथ कांपना, कभी-कभार फोकस हंटिंग, अपूर्ण फ्रेमिंग, प्राकृतिक ज़ूम समायोजन, सॉफ्ट टेप जैसी इमेज क्वालिटी, सूक्ष्म ग्रेन, खिड़की के पास की उज्ज्वल दिन की रोशनी से यथार्थवादी ऑटो-एक्सपोज़र बदलाव। प्राकृतिक स्किन टोन, हल्का मोशन ब्लर, पॉलिश किए गए सिनेमाई फुटेज के बजाय प्रामाणिक कंज्यूमर कैमकोर्डर सौंदर्य।
स्टाइल: कोमल ASMR तत्वों के साथ आरामदायक क्विक प्लांट-केयर व्लॉग। रिलैक्स्ड पेसिंग, न्यूनतम संवाद, स्पष्ट क्षण। संतोषजनक रोजमर्रा की आवाज़ों पर ध्यान: मिट्टी डालना, छोटे गमले को खुरचना, रसीले पत्तों का रगड़ना, धीरे से थपथपाना।
विषय: 20 के दशक के मध्य की एक युवती, सादा कार्डिगन, ढीले जूड़े में बाल, न्यूनतम गहने, कोई दृश्य लोगो या ब्रांडेड आइटम नहीं। एक छोटे से काम के लिए शांत, हंसमुख ऊर्जा।
सेटिंग: एक उज्ज्वल सुबह में छोटी रसोई की खिड़की। प्राकृतिक दिन का प्रकाश, एक सादा टेराकोटा गमला, मिट्टी का बिना निशान वाला बैग, फ्रेम में कोई दृश्य ब्रांड नाम या लोगो नहीं।
स्टोरीबोर्ड:
→ (3 सेकंड, प्रॉप किया गया मीडियम शॉट) कैमरे को सिल पर रखती है, एक छोटे गमले में मिट्टी डालती है। "इस छोटे से पौधे के लिए जल्दी से रिपॉटिंग।"
→ (3 सेकंड, क्लोज-अप) धीरे से रसीले पौधे को उसके पुराने गमले से ढीला करती है।
→ (3 सेकंड, हैंडहेल्ड शॉट) इसे ताजी मिट्टी में सेट करती है, आधार के चारों ओर थपथपाती है। "वहां, अब बहुत खुश है।"
→ (3 सेकंड, डिटेल शॉट) पत्तों से अतिरिक्त मिट्टी झाड़ती है। कोई संवाद नहीं।
→ (3 सेकंड, वार्म एंडिंग शॉट) इसे वापस सिल पर रखती है, कैमरे की ओर देखकर मुस्कुराती है। "अगले पौधे के साथ मिलते हैं।" रिकॉर्डिंग समाप्त होते ही हाथ लेंस को ढंक लेता है।
ऑडियो नोट्स: मिट्टी डालने और पत्तों को रगड़ने की प्राकृतिक परिवेशी आवाज़ें स्पष्ट रूप से सुनाई देनी चाहिए। संवाद शांत और अनौपचारिक, जैसे किसी छोटे व्यक्तिगत दर्शकों से बात कर रहे हों।
यथार्थवाद नोट्स: प्रामाणिक शारीरिक भाषा, स्वाभाविक पलक झपकना, सच्ची हंसमुख मुस्कान, अपूर्ण फ्रेमिंग, फोकस ब्रीदिंग, उज्ज्वल दिन के प्रकाश में बदलाव। फ्रेम में कहीं भी कॉपीराइट किए गए पात्र, लोगो, ब्रांड नाम या पहचानने योग्य सार्वजनिक हस्तियां नहीं हैं। पूरी तरह से मूल व्यक्तिगत व्लॉग सामग्री, न कि AI-जनरेटेड या व्यावसायिक शैली की।
```
